    $(document).on('click', '.destinal-form .hs-button', function(e) {
        var destinationVal=jQuery('select[name="destination"]').val();            
        if(destinationVal!=''){
            window.location=destinationVal;
        }              
        e.preventDefault();            
   });

   });

<span class="hs_cos_wrapper hs_cos_wrapper_widget hs_cos_wrapper_type_raw_html" data-hs-cos-general-type="widget" data-hs-cos-type="raw_html" id="hs_cos_wrapper_module_1503410602593285" style=""><div class="reveal-modal" id="myModal">
<p>Planning ahead for 2018 not only makes the process easier, but more fun as well! An internship abroad ensures you stand out from your peers and your newfound cultural competency proves to future employers that you're a valuable asset to the workplace.</p>
<p>Benefits of enrolling early:</p>
<ul>
<li>1. First to be presented to employers to secure a competitive internship. It's first come first serve!</li>
<li>2. Small monthly payments with our auto pay plan.</li>
<li>3. Have a plan for your summer - no extra stress during the school year.</li>
<li>4. Lock in 2017 pricing before 2018 prices are posted. Prices only go up!</li>
</ul>

<div class="cell-wrapper layout-widget-wrapper">
<span class="hs_cos_wrapper hs_cos_wrapper_widget hs_cos_wrapper_type_rich_text" data-hs-cos-general-type="widget" data-hs-cos-type="rich_text" id="hs_cos_wrapper_module_1468407020307104983" style=""><div class="bx-wrapper" style="max-width: 970px; margin: 0px auto;"><div class="bx-viewport" style="width: 100%; overflow: hidden; position: relative; height: 490px;"><div class="slider1" style="width: 815%; position: relative; transition-duration: 0s; transform: translate3d(-3900px, 0px, 0px);"><div class="slide bx-clone" style="float: left; list-style: none; position: relative; width: 770px; margin-right: 10px;">
<div class="slide-img"><img alt="Alexandria enjoying her time in Italy" sizes="(max-width: 250px) 100vw, 250px" src="https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=250&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg" srcset="https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=125&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 125w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=250&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 250w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=375&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 375w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=500&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 500w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=625&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 625w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/alexandria-testimonial.jpg?width=750&amp;name=alexandria-testimonial.jpg 750w" style="width: 250px;" width="250"/></div>
<div class="slide-content">
<p>I only have a few days left with my internship here in <strong>Florence</strong>, Italy, and I could not be more proud and surprised at what I have been able to accomplish in just 8 weeks. This internship has really pushed me past my comfort zone in the best way possible. And if you are thinking about interning abroad, do it! Just try! It has been one of my best decisions I've made ever!</p>
<h6>Alexandria H.</h6>
<p style="padding-left: 210px;"><span> University of Arkansas - Interior Design</span></p>
<span class="star-rating"><span class="fa fa-star" style="font-size: 16px; color: #f47e00;"></span><span class="fa fa-star" style="font-size: 16px; color: #f47e00;"></span><span class="fa fa-star" style="font-size: 16px; color: #f47e00;"></span><span class="fa fa-star" style="font-size: 16px; color: #f47e00;"></span><span class="fa fa-star" style="font-size: 16px; color: #f47e00;"></span></span></div>

<div class="slide" style="float: left; list-style: none; position: relative; width: 770px; margin-right: 10px;">
<div class="slide-img"><img alt="Benito after watching a soccer game" sizes="(max-width: 250px) 100vw, 250px" src="https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=250&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg" srcset="https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=125&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 125w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=250&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 250w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=375&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 375w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=500&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 500w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=625&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 625w, https://www.globalexperiences.com/hs-fs/hubfs/benito-testimonial.jpg?width=750&amp;name=benito-testimonial.jpg 750w" style="width: 250px;" width="250"/></div>
<div class="slide-content">
<p>What an experience!! I was a bit reluctant when I signed up for the <strong>London</strong> program, but Global Experience made it so easier and simple for me to navigate all the steps. All of this would have not been possible without all the GE coordinators that were here to guide us through the whole journey. 10/10 would recommend this program.</p>

<p>When I interned in <strong>Barcelona</strong> through the Global Experiences program the world opened up- it was like living in paradise and having the most fun of my entire life. With GE there were only positives and 100 percent I would recommend this program to anyone and everyone who wants to have a truly amazing experience.</p>

I promise you won't regret a single bit.</p>
